# Skills · A · part 17

866 entries, acceptance-check to access-report. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **acceptance-check**: Required functional proof for anything user-visible, run right after gate-runner. When a change touches UI or another externally visible surface, it reuses e2e coverage that already exists, or generates a focused Playwright check when none does; either way it captures screenshots, console output, and network errors, and writes verification-evidence.json per feature. Not for: unit/integration testing (gate-runner), changes with no externally visible surface, or replacing a full QA pass. ([kinnister/agentic-os](https://github.com/kinnister/agentic-os/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentic-sdlc/skills/acceptance-check) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **acceptance-check**: Use when asked to acceptance-test, verify, or validate that a feature implementation, branch, PR, or MR actually matches a spec, user story, ticket, PRD, or any requirements source. Triggers on "does this PR match the spec", "acceptance-test this", "verify the implementation against the ticket", "what's missing vs the requirements". Not for verifying the spec itself (use `verifying-requirements`) or authoring docs (use `docs-from-code`). ([similarweb/speclore](https://github.com/similarweb/speclore/tree/HEAD/skeleton/agents/cursor/.cursor/skills/acceptance-check) · ★2)
- **acceptance-check**: GitHub issue 本文の `## 受け入れ条件` セクションを抽出し、repo 状態や issue コメントを根拠に各項目を ✓ / ✗ / ? で報告する read-only skill。`issue-implement` と `issue-investigate` の受け入れ条件確認を標準化する。 ([hirokisakabe/issuekit](https://github.com/hirokisakabe/issuekit/tree/HEAD/skills/acceptance-check) · ★1)
- **acceptance-check**: Judge completion against requirements and test evidence. ([Tungdota53/vibe-studio](https://github.com/Tungdota53/vibe-studio/tree/HEAD/src/skills/acceptance-check) · ★1)
- **acceptance-check** ([EnigmaThe1/autonomous-factory-platform](https://github.com/EnigmaThe1/autonomous-factory-platform/tree/HEAD/skills/acceptance-check))
- **acceptance-check**: Turn a user story's acceptance criteria into an executable test checklist (happy/edge/negative/non-functional), traced back to each AC, with manual/auto + priority tags. Use when stories are approved and you need a QA acceptance plan, or when asked to "check what's testable". ([timur-dimukhametov/qa-agent-pipeline](https://github.com/timur-dimukhametov/qa-agent-pipeline/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/acceptance-check))
- **acceptance-check-execution**: Protocol for executing acceptance criteria verify commands from acceptance.json, distinguishing cmd versus manual checks, diagnosing authoring errors from real defects, and recording per-AC results. Use during QA verification or integration acceptance checks. ([JulianCissen/formrig](https://github.com/JulianCissen/formrig/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/acceptance-check-execution))
- **acceptance-checklist**: 基于 PRD 和交付包生成结构化验收清单，产出 docs/prd/&lt;feature-name&gt;/acceptance.md。按核心流程、边界与异常、UI 还原度、数据埋点四个维度拆解，每条标 P0/P1/P2 优先级并注明依据出处（引用 PRD 的需求编号）。适用于研发提测后的产品走查、上线前的功能验收；不用于写测试代码、执行自动化测试或判定代码质量。 ([mingdui/AI-Product-Skills](https://github.com/mingdui/AI-Product-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/acceptance-checklist) · ★13)
- **acceptance-checklist**: Generates a step-by-step user acceptance test (UAT) checklist that a real human executes by hand, with expected results and failure playbooks at every checkpoint. Covers CLI + Web UI scenarios, supports cold-start injection, and is checkbox-driven so the operator can mark progress. Trigger words: "/uat", "验收清单", "真人验收", "acceptance test", "让我验收". ([inernoro/prd_agent](https://github.com/inernoro/prd_agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/acceptance-checklist) · ★3)
- **acceptance-checklist**: Hand a finished change to the human for acceptance - a numbered checklist of what to open, do and expect, then the defect-fix loop over what they report back. Use when a pull request is ready for the user to verify, or the user asks what to check or watch out for (「我要驗收什麼」「有什麼需要特別注意」), or asks for the next step after a build or PR. ([EnderWolf50/skills](https://github.com/EnderWolf50/skills/tree/HEAD/workflow/acceptance-checklist))
- **acceptance-checklist**: 生成给真人手动执行的用户验收测试（UAT）清单：每个 checkpoint 都列出"敲什么命令 / 点哪个按钮 / 应该看到什么 / 看不到时按什么键排错"，覆盖后端接口、前端页面与混合场景，支持冷启动注入（Docker / MySQL / Redis / Nacos）和打勾驱动的进度跟踪。区别于 `/smoke`（curl 冒烟）和 `/handoff`（交接文档），本技能面向"亲手操作系统的真人"。触发词："/uat"、"验收清单"、"真人验收"、"acceptance test"、"让我验收"。 ([MiDouTech/myTapd](https://github.com/MiDouTech/myTapd/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/acceptance-checklist))
